### scenario.json配置： 定长 clod dp1 单前缀  
```
{
  "schema_version": "1.0",
  "run": {
    "run_id": "gsm8k-token2048-prefix-cache-70_8",
    "random_seed": 42,
    "output_dir": "./outputs/gsm8k-token2048-prefix-cache-70_8"
  },
  "tokenizer": {
    "path": "/home/weights/Qwen3-8B",
    "block_size": 128,
    "trust_remote_code": false
  },
  "corpus": {
    "path": "ais_bench/datasets/gsm8k/test.jsonl",
    "field": "question",
    "selection": {
      "mode": "random"
    }
  },
  "requests": {
    "count": 20,
    "input_length": {"mode": "fixed", "value": 1024},
    "output_length": {"mode": "fixed", "value": 1024}
  },
  "output": {
    "output_key": null
  },
  "prefix_cache": {
    "mode": "cold",
    "target_hit_rate": 0.8,
    "seed_blocks": 1,
    "groups": {"count": 1, "assignment": {"mode": "uniform"}},
    "order": {"strategy": "sequential"}
  },
  "service": {
    "inference_url": "http://127.0.0.1:8005/v1/completions",
    "metrics_url": "http://127.0.0.1:8005/metrics",
    "reset_url": "http://127.0.0.1:8005/reset_prefix_cache",
    "model": "qwen3",
    "dp_size": 1,
    "assume_empty_cache": false
  },
  "validation": {"target_warning_pp": 1.0, "actual_warning_pp": 5.0},
  "aisbench": {"config": "./prefix_cache_perf.py", "work_dir": "./outputs/aisbench", "extra_args": ["--num-warmups", "0"]}
}

```

### ais-bench-prefix-cache run --scenario ./scenario.json

### 多dp warmup 截断分布，固定长度，多组前缀，长度按照从短到长发送

```
{
  "schema_version": "1.0",
  "run": {
    "run_id": "gsm8k-token2048-prefix-cache-70_8",
    "random_seed": 42,
    "output_dir": "./outputs/gsm8k-token2048-prefix-cache-70_8"
  },
  "tokenizer": {
    "path": "/home/weights/Qwen3-8B",
    "block_size": 128,
    "trust_remote_code": false
  },
  "corpus": {
    "path": "ais_bench/datasets/gsm8k/test.jsonl",
    "field": "question",
    "selection": {
      "mode": "random"
    }
  },
  "requests": {
    "count": 20,
    "input_length": {
      "mode": "range",
      "ranges": [
        {"min": 512, "max": 1024, "count": 20}
      ]
    },
    "output_length": {"mode": "fixed", "value": 1024}
  },
  "output": {
    "output_key": null
  },
  "prefix_cache": {
    "mode": "warmup",
    "target_hit_rate": 0.6,
    "seed_blocks": 1,
    "minimum_non_shared_length": 128,
    "groups": {"count": 2, "assignment": {"mode": "uniform"}},
    "order": {"strategy": "input_len_asc"}
  },
  "service": {
    "inference_url": "http://127.0.0.1:8005/v1/completions",
    "metrics_url": "http://127.0.0.1:8005/metrics",
    "reset_url": "http://127.0.0.1:8005/reset_prefix_cache",
    "model": "qwen3",
    "dp_size": 2,
    "assume_empty_cache": false,
    "engine_label_map": {},
    "timeout_seconds": 30,
    "api_key": "",
    "poll_interval_seconds": 5.0
  },
  "validation": {"target_warning_pp": 1.0, "actual_warning_pp": 5.0},
  "aisbench": {
    "config": "./plugins/prefix_cache/config_examples/prefix_cache_perf.py",
    "work_dir": "./outputs/aisbench-prefix-cache-60",
    "extra_args": [],
    "dataset": {
      "abbr": null,
      "input_columns": ["question", "max_out_len"],
      "output_column": "answer",
      "prompt_template": "{question}",
      "pred_role": "BOT"
    },
    "model": {
      "attr": "service",
      "abbr": null,
      "stream": true,
      "retry": 2,
      "batch_size": 2,
      "generation_kwargs": {
        "temperature": 0,
        "ignore_eos": true
      }
    }
  }
}
```
